;;   它挂在 org-mode hook 上, 每开一个 org 文件就加载一次, 而且本机没装

* Org Clipboard & DnD
** Screenshot / rich paste helpers
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(defun my/org-download-screenshot-command ()
  "Platform-appropriate screenshot command for org-download."
  (cond
   ((featurep :system 'windows)
    "powershell -Command \"Add-Type -AssemblyName System.Windows.Forms; $img = [System.Windows.Forms.Clipboard]::GetImage(); if ($img) { $img.Save('%s', [System.Drawing.Imaging.ImageFormat]::Png) } else { Write-Error 'No image in clipboard' }\"")
   ((featurep :system 'macos)
    "sh -c 'if command -v pngpaste >/dev/null 2>&1 && pngpaste \"$1\" >/dev/null 2>&1; then exit 0; else screencapture -i \"$1\"; fi' _ %s")
   (t
    ;; 链条: 剪贴板里的图 -> flameshot 选区 -> gnome-screenshot 选区 -> maim/grim(Wayland 老机型)
    ;; 注意: 每一步都要求上一步产出的文件为空才继续, 避免覆盖已有截图。
    "sh -c 'if command -v xclip >/dev/null 2>&1; then xclip -selection clipboard -t image/png -o > \"$1\" 2>/dev/null || true; fi; if [ ! -s \"$1\" ]; then if command -v wl-paste >/dev/null 2>&1; then wl-paste --no-newline --type image/png > \"$1\" 2>/dev/null || true; fi; fi; if [ ! -s \"$1\" ]; then if command -v flameshot >/dev/null 2>&1; then flameshot gui -r > \"$1\" 2>/dev/null || true; fi; fi; if [ ! -s \"$1\" ]; then if command -v gnome-screenshot >/dev/null 2>&1; then gnome-screenshot -a -f \"$1\"; fi; fi; if [ ! -s \"$1\" ]; then if command -v maim >/dev/null 2>&1; then maim -s \"$1\"; elif command -v grim >/dev/null 2>&1 && command -v slurp >/dev/null 2>&1; then grim -g \"$(slurp)\" \"$1\"; fi; fi' _ %s")))

;; 2026-10-17: 原 `my/org-download-denote-file-format' 改名为
;; `my/org-file-name-format' 并去掉 denote 依赖，实现在文件前部的「文件名生成」块。
;; 调用方（org-attach 命名、markdown DnD）已全部改成新名字。

(defun my/org-paste-rich ()
  "Paste rich text (HTML with images) from clipboard as Org content."
  (interactive)
  (unless buffer-file-name
    (user-error "Please save the current buffer first"))
  (pcase system-type
    ('windows-nt
     ;; 2026-10-20: 跟 Linux 分支同构 —— 图落进当前 entry 的 org-attach 目录,
     ;; 命名 <ts>--<slug>.ext, 链接写成 [[attachment:…]]。
     ;; 脚本默认把链接写成硬编码的 `file:.images/…', 所以用 -LinkPrefix 覆盖成
     ;; **绝对路径**, 回来再交给 `my/org-html--rename-extracted' 统一收拾。
     ;; ⚠ 这里做不到 Linux 那种 lazy: 剪贴板内容是 PowerShell 那边才看得到的,
     ;;    Emacs 侧要探测就得再起一次进程。代价是粘纯文本也会给 heading 发个 ID。
     (let* ((img-dir (org-attach-dir-get-create))
            (script (expand-file-name "~/org/.src/clipboard-to-org.ps1"))
            (img-dir-win (replace-regexp-in-string "/" "\\\\" img-dir))
            (script-win (replace-regexp-in-string "/" "\\\\" script))
            (link-prefix (concat "file:" (file-name-as-directory img-dir)))
            (cmd (format "powershell.exe -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File \"%s\" -ImageDir \"%s\" -LinkPrefix \"%s\""
                         script-win img-dir-win link-prefix))
            (out-file (string-trim (shell-command-to-string cmd))))
       (if (and (not (string-blank-p out-file))
                (file-exists-p out-file))
           (let* ((text (with-temp-buffer
                          (let ((coding-system-for-read 'utf-8))
                            (insert-file-contents out-file))
                          (buffer-string)))
                  (r (my/org-html--rename-extracted text img-dir)))
             (delete-file out-file)
             (insert (car r))
             (my/org-display-images)
             (message "OK: rich text pasted (%d image(s) -> %s)"
                      (cdr r) (abbreviate-file-name img-dir)))
         (message "WARN: clipboard empty or conversion failed (out: %s)" out-file))))
    ('darwin
     (let* ((script (expand-file-name "~/org/.src/clipboard-to-org-macos.sh"))
            (img-dir (expand-file-name ".images" (file-name-directory buffer-file-name))))
       (cond
        ((file-exists-p script)
         (let ((out-file (string-trim (shell-command-to-string
                                       (format "sh %s %s" (shell-quote-argument script) (shell-quote-argument img-dir))))))
           (if (and (not (string-blank-p out-file))
                    (file-exists-p out-file))
               (progn
                 (insert-file-contents out-file)
                 (delete-file out-file)
                 (message "OK: rich text pasted"))
             (message "WARN: macOS clipboard conversion failed"))))
        ((executable-find "pbpaste")
         (let ((text (shell-command-to-string "pbpaste")))
           (if (string-blank-p text)
               (message "WARN: macOS clipboard empty")
             (insert text)
             (message "OK: plain text pasted (macOS fallback)"))))
        (t (message "WARN: pbpaste not found")))))
    ('gnu/linux (my/org-paste-rich-linux))
    (_ (message "WARN: rich text clipboard not implemented for this platform"))))

(defun my/org-clipboard-html ()
  "Return HTML from the clipboard (X11/Wayland), or nil."
  (cond
   ((executable-find "xclip")
    (let ((s (shell-command-to-string "xclip -selection clipboard -t text/html -o 2>/dev/null")))
      (unless (string-blank-p s) s)))
   ((executable-find "wl-paste")
    (let ((s (shell-command-to-string "wl-paste --no-newline --type text/html 2>/dev/null")))
      (unless (string-blank-p s) s)))))

(defun my/org-clipboard-text ()
  "Return plain text from the clipboard (Linux), or nil."
  (let ((s (cond ((executable-find "xclip")
                  (shell-command-to-string "xclip -selection clipboard -o 2>/dev/null"))
                 ((executable-find "wl-paste")
                  (shell-command-to-string "wl-paste --no-newline 2>/dev/null")))))
    (unless (string-blank-p s) s)))

(defun my/org-html--captions (text)
  "Pandoc 输出的 `#+CAPTION:` 与图片链接的配对表: ((文件名 . 说明) …)。
只有 <figure><figcaption> 才有 caption; 光秃秃的 <img> 不在表里,
调用方回落到 `image-N'。"
  (let (out)
    (with-temp-buffer
      (insert text)
      (goto-char (point-min))
      (while (re-search-forward
              "^[ \t]*#\\+CAPTION:[ \t]*\\(.+\\)[ \t]*\n[ \t]*\\[\\[\\(?:file:\\)?[^]]*/\\([^]/]+\\)\\]\\]"
              nil t)
        (push (cons (match-string-no-properties 2)
                    (string-trim (match-string-no-properties 1)))
              out)))
    out))

(defun my/org-html--slug (cap fallback)
  "把 caption 文本 CAP 洗成能进文件名的 slug; 洗完是空就用 FALLBACK。
CJK 保留 (本库附件名本来就是 CJK), 只替换文件系统和 shell 里会出事的那几个字符。
截到 40 列宽: 时间戳 15 字符 + `--` + slug + 扩展名, 别顶到 ext4 的 255 字节上限。"
  (let ((out (string-trim
              (replace-regexp-in-string "[/\\\\:*?\"<>|\r\n\t]+" "-" (or cap "")))))
    (if (string-blank-p out)
        fallback
      (truncate-string-to-width out 40 nil nil t))))

(defun my/org-html--rename-extracted (text img-dir)
  "重命名 IMG-DIR 里 pandoc 抽出的图, 并改写 TEXT 里的链接。
pandoc 把内联图抽成 `<hash>.<ext>` 直接放在 IMG-DIR 下 —— 那个 hash 换
pandoc 版本就变, 不能当长期名字。这里按本库约定改成 `<时间戳>--<slug>.<ext>`,
链接整段换成 `[[attachment:<新名>]]` —— 不写死路径。
返回 (新文本 . 重命名条数)。"
  (let* ((prefix (regexp-quote (directory-file-name img-dir)))
         (caps (my/org-html--captions text))
         ;; 时间戳前缀由目录名拼回来: 目录是 <ID 前6>/<ID 余下>,
         ;; 于是「目录名 == 文件名前缀」, 跟日记那批完全同构。
         ;; ⚠ 不能在这里调 org-entry-get —— 本函数是在 pandoc 输出的
         ;; **临时 buffer** 里跑的, 那里没有 entry, 取出来永远是 nil
         ;; (2026-10-20 踩到: 所有图都落成当前时间而非 entry ID)。
         (ts (let ((rel (file-relative-name (file-truename img-dir)
                                            (file-truename org-attach-id-dir))))
              (if (string-match-p "\\`[0-9]\\{6\\}/[^/]+\\'" rel)
                  (replace-regexp-in-string "/" "" rel)
                (format-time-string "%Y%m%dT%H%M%S"))))
         (refs nil) (i 0) (n 0))
    ;; 只认 text 里引用过的图 —— pandoc 只引用它自己抽出的媒体,
    ;; 远程 http 图它不动, 因此不会进这张表。
    (with-temp-buffer
      (insert text)
      (goto-char (point-min))
      (while (re-search-forward
              (concat "\\[\\[\\(?:file:\\)?" prefix "/\\([^]]+\\)\\]\\]") nil t)
        (let ((ref (match-string-no-properties 1)))
          (unless (assoc ref refs)
            (push (cons ref (file-name-nondirectory ref)) refs)))))
    (setq refs (nreverse refs))
    (dolist (r refs)
      (setq i (1+ i))
      (let* ((ref (car r))
             (name (cdr r))
             (ext (or (file-name-extension name) "bin"))
             (slug (my/org-html--slug (cdr (assoc name caps))
                                      (format "image-%d" i)))
             (new-name (format "%s--%s.%s" ts slug ext))
             (old-path (expand-file-name name img-dir))
             (new-path (expand-file-name new-name img-dir))
             (k 1))
        (while (file-exists-p new-path)   ; 同一秒粘了两次同样的图
          (setq new-name (format "%s--%s-%d.%s" ts slug k ext))
          (setq new-path (expand-file-name new-name img-dir))
          (setq k (1+ k)))
        (when (file-exists-p old-path)
          (rename-file old-path new-path t)
          (setq n (1+ n))
          ;; ⚠ 必须整段 [[…]] 一起换, 不能只换中间的路径: pandoc 输出的链接
          ;; 可能是 [[file:/abs/path/x.png]] 也可能是裸的 [[/abs/path/x.png]],
          ;; 只换路径会留下 [[file:attachment:x.png]] 这种畸形链接
          ;; (2026-10-20 踩到, 第一次单测只用了裸路径形态所以没暴露)。
          ;; prefix 已经是 regexp-quote 过的。
          (setq text (replace-regexp-in-string
                      (concat "\\[\\[\\(?:file:\\)?" prefix "/" (regexp-quote ref) "\\]\\]")
                      (concat "[[attachment:" new-name "]]")
                      text t t)))))
    (cons text n)))

(defun my/org-html-to-org (html img-dir)
  "Convert HTML string to Org with pandoc, extracting images into IMG-DIR.
Returns the Org text, or nil on failure.

2026-10-20 收口: IMG-DIR 由调用方传 `org-attach-dir-get-create' —— per-entry
目录, 不再是笔记同级的 `.images/'。pandoc 抽出的 hash 名会被重命名成
`<时间戳>--<slug>.<ext>`, 链接一律写成 `[[attachment:<新名>]]`。
理由: 写死路径 = 锁定布局; hash 名换 pandoc 版本就变, 而且读不懂。"
  (unless (executable-find "pandoc")
    (user-error "pandoc not found (Arch: pacman -S pandoc-cli; Debian: apt install pandoc)"))
  (when img-dir (make-directory img-dir t))
  (let* ((tmp (make-temp-file "org-clip" nil ".html"))
         (out (concat (file-name-sans-extension tmp) ".org")))
    (unwind-protect
        (progn
          (let ((coding-system-for-write 'utf-8))
            (write-region html nil tmp nil 'silent))
          (and (zerop (apply #'call-process
                             "pandoc" nil nil nil
                             (append
                              ;; auto_identifiers 会给每个标题加 :PROPERTIES: 抽屉, 关掉更干净
                              (list "-f" "html-auto_identifiers" "-t" "org"
                                    "--wrap=none")
                              (when img-dir
                                (list (concat "--extract-media=" img-dir)))
                              (list tmp "-o" out))))
               (with-temp-buffer
                 (let ((coding-system-for-read 'utf-8))
                   (insert-file-contents out))
                 (org-mode)
                 (let ((text (string-trim (buffer-string))))
                   ;; ── 1) svgz: 知乎把图标存成 .svgz, 但内容其实是纯 SVG 文本而非
                   ;;    gzip -> Emacs 不认这个后缀, 整批不显示。检测 magic number 改后缀。
                   (dolist (old (and img-dir
                                     (directory-files img-dir t "\\.svgz\\'" t)))
                     (let ((new (concat (file-name-sans-extension old) ".svg")))
                       ;; 用 -literally 读: 普通的 insert-file-contents 会被
                       ;; auto-compression-mode 先解压, 那就永远读不到 gzip magic 了。
                       (unless (equal (with-temp-buffer
                                        (set-buffer-multibyte nil)
                                        (insert-file-contents-literally old nil 0 2)
                                        (buffer-string))
                                      "\x1f\x8b")
                         (rename-file old new t)
                         (setq text (replace-regexp-in-string
                                     (regexp-quote (file-name-nondirectory old))
                                     (file-name-nondirectory new)
                                     text t t)))))
                   ;; ── 2) hash 名 -> <ts>--<slug>.<ext>, 链接 -> attachment:
                   (let ((r (my/org-html--rename-extracted text img-dir)))
                     (setq text (car r)))
                   ;; ── 3) 剩下的裸相对路径(远程图, 没有 scheme) 补 file: 前缀 ——
                   ;;    org 会把 [[x.png]] 当 **fuzzy** 链接, 内联图一张都不显示
                   ;;    (2026-10-10 踩到)。用 [^][:] 排除 http(s): 这类带 type 的,
                   ;;    再用 [^/:] 打头排除 // 协议相对 URL 和绝对路径。
                   (setq text
                         (replace-regexp-in-string
                          "\\[\\[\\([^][:/][^][:]*\\.\\(?:png\\|jpe?g\\|gif\\|webp\\|tiff?\\|svgz?\\|bmp\\)\\)\\]\\]"
                          "[[file:\\1]]"
                          text t nil))
                   text))))
      (ignore-errors (delete-file tmp))
      (ignore-errors (delete-file out)))))

(defun my/org-paste-rich-linux ()
  "Paste clipboard HTML as Org content (Linux / X11 + Wayland).
内联图落到当前 entry 的附件目录 (`org-attach-dir-get-create'), 命名是
`<时间戳>--<slug>.<ext>`, 链接写成 `[[attachment:…]]`。远程 http 图保持链接
—— 想落地就用 `my/org-image-download-link-at-point'。"
  (let* ((html (my/org-clipboard-html))
         ;; lazy: 只有真有图才去建目录 / 给 heading 造 ID,
         ;;       免得粘一段纯文本就给每个 heading 都发个 ID
         (img-dir (and html
                       (string-match-p "<img\\|data:image" html)
                       (org-attach-dir-get-create))))
    (if (or (null html) (string-blank-p html))
        (insert (or (my/org-clipboard-text) ""))
      (let ((org-text (my/org-html-to-org html img-dir)))
        (unless org-text
          (user-error "pandoc conversion failed"))
        (insert org-text)
        (my/org-display-images)
        (message "OK: rich text pasted (images -> %s)"
                 (if img-dir
                     (abbreviate-file-name img-dir)
                   "(none)"))))))

(defvar my/org-dedupe-script "~/.local/bin/org-dedupe-images.py"
  "感知哈希去重脚本(需要 python 的 PIL)。见 `my/org-dedupe-inline-images'。")

(defun my/org-dedupe-inline-images (&optional apply)
  "删除本 buffer 里「相邻重复」的内联图片, 保留分辨率更高的那份。
知乎这类站点在 HTML 里同时给了缩略图(jpg)和原图(webp), pandoc 会把两份都
抽出来 -> 笔记里就出现一对长得一模一样的图(2026-10-10 踩到: 56 张其实只有
28 张)。判定用 dHash 感知哈希, 默认只报告(dry run), \\`C-u' 前缀才真的改文件;
冗余图片走 gio trash 进回收站(可恢复), 不直接删。"
  (interactive "P")
  (unless buffer-file-name
    (user-error "Buffer 还没存盘, 先保存"))
  (let ((script (expand-file-name my/org-dedupe-script)))
    (unless (file-exists-p script)
      (user-error "缺少去重脚本 %s" script))
    (save-buffer)
    (let ((out-buf (get-buffer-create "*Org dedupe*")))
      (with-current-buffer out-buf (erase-buffer))
      (apply #'call-process (or (executable-find "python3") "python3")
             nil out-buf t
             (append (list script buffer-file-name)
                     (and apply (list "--apply"))))
      (when apply
        (revert-buffer t t)
        (when (fboundp 'org-link-preview-clear) (org-link-preview-clear))
        (my/org-display-images))
      (display-buffer out-buf))))
#+end_src

** org-download
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(after! org
  (use-package! org-download
    :commands (org-download-clipboard org-download-screenshot org-download-yank org-download-delete)
    :config
    ;; ⚠️ 不要 org-download-enable: 它会把 org-download-dnd 塞到
    ;; dnd-protocol-alist 最前面 (且是 setq 不是 setq-local, 会盖掉 org-mode
    ;; 自己在 org-setup-yank-dnd-handlers 里装的 handler), 拖放就绕过
    ;; org-yank-dnd-method 了。org-download 的其它命令 (clipboard/screenshot/
    ;; yank/delete) 不依赖这个 hook, 照样能用。
    ;; (add-hook 'org-mode-hook 'org-download-enable)
    (setq org-download-method 'attach     ; ← 统一收口: 落 org-attach-dir
          ;; attach 模式下 org-download 自己会 (let ((org-download-image-dir
          ;; (org-attach-dir t))) ...) 覆盖, 这行只是非 org buffer 的兜底
          org-download-image-dir (expand-file-name "data/" org-directory)
          org-download-heading-lvl nil
          org-download-timestamp ""
          ;; 0 = 不写 #+attr_org: :width (org-download 内部是 (= width 0) 判断,
          ;; 写 nil 会 wrong-type-argument)。宽度交给 org-image-max-width 统一管。
          org-download-image-org-width 0
          org-download-annotate-function (lambda (_link) "")
          org-download-screenshot-method (my/org-download-screenshot-command)
          ;; 命名跟 org-attach 走同一套 (可读名保留 / 机器名转 denote 名)
          org-download-file-format-function #'my/org-attach-name-for-file)
          ;; ⚠️ 原来这里覆盖了 org-download-link-format-function 写死 [[file:]]。
          ;; attach 模式下必须去掉, 让默认的 org-download-link-format-function-default
          ;; 吐出 [[attachment:name]], 否则又变成扁平 file: 链接。

    ;; Fix: org-download-dnd-fallback for Emacs 30+
    (when (fboundp 'dnd-handle-multiple-urls)
      (defun org-download-dnd-fallback (uri action)
        (let ((dnd-protocol-alist
               (rassq-delete-all
                'org-download-dnd
                (copy-alist dnd-protocol-alist))))
          (dnd-handle-multiple-urls
           (selected-window) (list uri) action))))

    ;; Fix: Full percent-decoding for non-ASCII filenames + Windows local paths.
    (defun org-download--fullname (link &optional ext)
      "Return the file name where LINK will be saved to.
[patched] Robust Windows path handling + full percent-decoding."
      (let ((filename
             (decode-coding-string
              (url-unhex-string
               (file-name-nondirectory
                (if (file-name-absolute-p link)
                    link
                  (or (car (url-path-and-query
                            (url-generic-parse-url link)))
                      link))))
              'utf-8))
            (dir (org-download--dir)))
        (when (string-match ".*?\\.\\(?:png\\|jpg\\)\\(.*\\)$" filename)
          (setq filename (replace-match "" nil nil filename 1)))
        (when ext
          (setq filename (concat (file-name-sans-extension filename) "." ext)))
        (abbreviate-file-name
         (expand-file-name
          (funcall org-download-file-format-function filename)
          dir))))

    ;; Fix: org-download-clipboard on Windows ignores user's screenshot-method.
    (when (featurep :system 'windows)
      (defadvice! my/org-download-clipboard-use-powershell (&optional basename)
        :override #'org-download-clipboard
        (org-id-get-create)
        (org-download-screenshot basename)))))
#+end_src

** Delete file link in Org
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
;; 用 org-element-context 解析链接, 支持 file:/attachment: 类型及带描述的 [[file:x.png][caption]]
(defun my/org-delete-file-link-at-point ()
  "Delete the file link at point and the underlying attached file."
  (interactive)
  (let* ((context (org-element-context))
         (type (org-element-property :type context)))
    (unless (member type '("file" "attachment"))
      (user-error "Not on a file or attachment link"))
    ;; NOTE: 这里原来拿 org-attach-id-dir 当 attachment 的基准目录, 但真实目录是
    ;; data/<ID前2位>/<ID余下>/, 结果 attachment 链接全被判成「文件已不存在」
    ;; 而链接照样被删掉。统一走 my/org-link-file-at-point。
    (let* ((abspath (or (my/org-link-file-at-point)
                        (user-error "Cannot resolve the file at point")))
           (beg (org-element-property :begin context))
           (end (org-element-property :end context))
           (result (my/delete-file-link--try-delete abspath)))
      (when result
        (delete-region beg end)
        (when (and (looking-at "[ \t]*$") (not (bolp)))
          (delete-region (point) (line-end-position)))
        (message (if (eq result 'deleted)
                     "Deleted file and removed link"
                   "Removed link (file was already missing"))))))
#+end_src

** Image helpers: external open / inline resize / caption
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
;;; my/org-image-file-at-point
(defun my/org-image-file-at-point ()
  "Return the absolute path of the image file linked at point, or nil."
  (let* ((ctx  (org-element-context))
         (type (org-element-property :type ctx))
         (path (when (member type '("file" "attachment"))
                 (org-element-property :path ctx))))
    (when path
      ;; attachment: 的基准目录是 (org-attach-dir), 不是 org-attach-id-dir
      (let ((file (my/org-link-file-at-point)))
        (when (and file (file-regular-p file)
                   (member (downcase (or (file-name-extension file) ""))
                           '("png" "jpg" "jpeg" "gif" "webp" "svg" "tiff" "bmp")))
          file)))))

;;; my/org-image-open-external
;; Windows-only API, Linux 下不存在, 声明一下避免 byte-compile 警告
(declare-function w32-shell-execute "w32fns" (operation &optional parameters directory show-flag))
(defun my/org-image-open-external ()
  "Open the image at point with the OS default viewer."
  (interactive)
  (if-let ((file (my/org-image-file-at-point)))
      (progn
        (if (eq system-type 'windows-nt)
            (w32-shell-execute "open" file)
          (call-process "xdg-open" nil 0 nil file))
        (message "Opened %s" (file-name-nondirectory file)))
    (user-error "No image file at point")))

;;; my/org-image-resize-at-point
(defun my/org-image--overlay-at-point ()
  "Return the inline-image overlay covering point, or nil."
  (let ((ovs (or (and (boundp 'org-link-preview-overlays) org-link-preview-overlays)
                 (cl-remove-if-not (lambda (ov) (imagep (overlay-get ov 'display)))
                                   (overlays-in (point-min) (point-max))))))
    (cl-find-if (lambda (ov)
                  (and (<= (overlay-start ov) (point))
                       (< (point) (overlay-end ov))))
                ovs)))

(defun my/org-image-resize-at-point (&optional delta)
  "Resize the inline image at point by DELTA percent (default +10).
Only changes the display width; the file on disk is untouched.  A redisplay
\(e.g. toggling `org-toggle-inline-images' twice) restores the default width."
  (interactive "P")
  (let* ((ov  (my/org-image--overlay-at-point))
         (img (and ov (overlay-get ov 'display)))
         (cur (and img (or (plist-get (cdr img) :width)
                           (ignore-errors (car (image-size img t))))))
         (pct (cond ((null delta) 0.1)
                    ((numberp delta) (/ (float delta) 100.0))
                    (t (/ (float (read-number "Zoom by % (negative = shrink): " 10)) 100.0)))))
    (unless cur
      (user-error "No inline image at point — turn it on with SPC m i t first"))
    (let* ((new  (max 40 (round (* (+ 1.0 pct) cur))))
           (spec (cons 'image (plist-put (copy-sequence (cdr img)) :width new))))
      (overlay-put ov 'display spec)
      (message "Inline image width: %dpx" new))))

(defun my/org-image-resize-up () (interactive) (my/org-image-resize-at-point 10))
(defun my/org-image-resize-down () (interactive) (my/org-image-resize-at-point -10))

;;; my/org-image-caption
(defun my/org-image-caption ()
  "Add a #+CAPTION line above the image at point, leaving point at its end.
Re-run on an already-captioned image to append to the existing caption.
Implemented with plain buffer scanning instead of `org-element-context',
whose  :begin  points to the wrong line right after the buffer was edited."
  (interactive)
  (unless (my/org-image-file-at-point)
    (user-error "No image file at point"))
  (let ((beg (line-beginning-position))
        ;; 坑: '#+' 里的 + 是字面字符, 必须转义成 #\\+, 否则被当成量词永远匹配不上
        (re  "#\\+\\(CAPTION\\|ATTR_\\|NAME\\|HEADER\\)"))
    ;; 向上吞掉连续的 affiliated 关键字行, 得到整个块的起始行。
    ;; 坑: forward-line 会试图保留列位置, 每次之后都要显式 beginning-of-line。
    (save-excursion
      (goto-char beg)
      (forward-line -1)
      (beginning-of-line)
      (while (and (not (bobp)) (looking-at-p re))
        (setq beg (point))
        (forward-line -1)
        (beginning-of-line)))
    (goto-char beg)
    (unless (looking-at-p "#\\+CAPTION:")
      (insert "#+CAPTION: \n")
      (forward-line -1))
    (end-of-line)
    (message "Caption ready")))
#+end_src

** Image backends: ImageMagick / Pillow
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(require 'cl-lib)

;; org 9.8 把这两个函数标成废弃, 新名分别是 org-link-preview-region / -refresh。
;; 包一层 fboundp 判断, 免得哪天切回内置 org 9.6 就炸。
(defun my/org-display-images ()
  "Display inline images (org 9.8+ name: `org-link-preview-region')."
  (if (fboundp 'org-link-preview-region)
      (org-link-preview-region)
    ;; 只在没有新 API 的老 org 上才会走到这里
    (with-no-warnings (org-display-inline-images))))

(defun my/org-redisplay-images ()
  "Refresh inline images (org 9.8+ name: `org-link-preview-refresh')."
  (if (fboundp 'org-link-preview-refresh)
      (org-link-preview-refresh)
    (with-no-warnings (org-redisplay-inline-images))))

(declare-function org--create-inline-image "ol" (file width))
(defvar org-link-preview-overlays)         ; org 9.8: defvar-local in ol.el
(defvar my/org-image-auto-refresh t
  "When non-nil, re-render shown inline images after the window width changes.")
(defvar my/org-image-auto-refresh-delay 0.8
  "Idle seconds to wait before re-rendering images after a resize.")
(defvar my/org-image-auto-refresh-max 40
  "Do not auto refresh a buffer holding more than this many image overlays.")
(defvar my/org-image--resize-timer nil)
(defvar my/org-image--resize-frame nil)
(defvar my/org-image--resize-sig nil)

(defun my/org-image-refresh-displayed ()
  "Re-render the inline images already displayed in the current buffer.
Only existing previews are rebuilt: the old :file and :width are fed back
into org so `org-image-max-width' is recomputed for the current window
width.  No new preview is ever added, which is what makes this safe to run
on a big buffer -- unlike `org-link-preview-refresh', which calls
`org-link-preview-region' over the whole buffer and would decode every
linked image again."
  (interactive)
  (unless (derived-mode-p 'org-mode)
    (user-error "Not in an org buffer"))
  (let ((ovs (bound-and-true-p org-link-preview-overlays))
        (count 0))
    (if (> (length ovs) my/org-image-auto-refresh-max)
        (message "Auto refresh skipped: %d image overlays (> %d)"
                 (length ovs) my/org-image-auto-refresh-max)
      (dolist (ov ovs)
        (let* ((spec  (overlay-get ov 'display))
               (props (and (consp spec) (cdr spec)))
               (file  (plist-get props :file))
               ;; 保留手动缩放过的 :width; 没有则交给 org-image-max-width 重算
               (width (plist-get props :width)))
          (when (and (stringp file) (file-exists-p file))
            (let ((new (and (fboundp 'org--create-inline-image)
                            (ignore-errors (org--create-inline-image file width)))))
              (when new
                (overlay-put ov 'display new)
                (setq count (1+ count)))))))
      (when (> count 0)
        (message "Re-rendered %d inline image(s) for the new width" count)))))

(defun my/org-image--resize-refresh-do ()
  (setq my/org-image--resize-timer nil)
  (let ((frame my/org-image--resize-frame))
    (when (frame-live-p frame)
      (dolist (win (window-list frame 'no-minibuf))
        ;; with-selected-window 是关键: org--create-inline-image 算 :max-width 时用的是
        ;; (window-width nil t), 也就是「当时 selected 的那个 window」。不切过去的话,
        ;; 并排窗口里刷新另一侧的 buffer 会拿错宽度(实测差了一倍)。
        (with-selected-window win
          (when (and (derived-mode-p 'org-mode)
                     (bound-and-true-p org-link-preview-overlays))
            (my/org-image-refresh-displayed)))))))

(defun my/org-image-refresh-after-window-resize (&optional frame)
  "Re-render shown inline images once FRAME's windows stop changing size."
  (let* ((frame (or frame (selected-frame)))
         ;; 只关心宽度: 单纯上下拖动窗口不该触发重解码
         (sig (when (frame-live-p frame)
                (cons (frame-pixel-width frame)
                      (window-width (frame-selected-window frame) t)))))
    (when (and my/org-image-auto-refresh sig
               (not (equal sig my/org-image--resize-sig)))
      (setq my/org-image--resize-sig sig
            my/org-image--resize-frame frame)
      (when (timerp my/org-image--resize-timer)
        (cancel-timer my/org-image--resize-timer))
      (setq my/org-image--resize-timer
            (run-with-idle-timer my/org-image-auto-refresh-delay nil
                                 #'my/org-image--resize-refresh-do)))))

(add-hook 'window-size-change-functions #'my/org-image-refresh-after-window-resize)

(defvar my/org-image--python nil "Cached Python executable that has Pillow.")
(defun my/org-image-python (&optional refresh)
  "Return a Python interpreter with Pillow installed, or nil.
Order: Windows scoop python311 (beancount env) -> python3 -> python.
Result is cached; call with REFRESH to probe again."
  (if (and my/org-image--python (not refresh))
      my/org-image--python
    (setq my/org-image--python
          (cl-find-if (lambda (p)
                        (and (stringp p) (file-executable-p p)
                             (zerop (call-process p nil nil nil "-c" "import PIL"))))
                      (list (expand-file-name "scoop/apps/python311/current/python.exe" "~")
                            "/usr/bin/python3"
                            (executable-find "python3")
                            (executable-find "python"))))))

(defun my/org-image-magick ()
  "Return an ImageMagick executable, or nil."
  (or (executable-find "magick")
      (cl-find-if (lambda (p) (file-executable-p p))
                  (list (expand-file-name "scoop/apps/imagemagick/current/magick.exe" "~")
                        "C:/ProgramData/scoop/apps/imagemagick/current/magick.exe"
                        "C:/PROGRA~3/scoop/apps/imagemagick/current/magick.exe"))))

(defconst my/org-image-pil-rotate
  "import sys
from PIL import Image, ImageOps
f, a = sys.argv[1], int(sys.argv[2])
im = ImageOps.exif_transpose(Image.open(f))
im.rotate(-a, expand=True).save(f)
"
  "Pillow rotation script. -a because PIL rotates counter-clockwise
while `mogrify -rotate N` goes clockwise — keeps both backends identical.")

(defconst my/org-image-pil-optimize
  "import sys
from PIL import Image, ImageOps
f, out, ms, q = sys.argv[1], sys.argv[2], int(sys.argv[3]), int(sys.argv[4])
im = ImageOps.exif_transpose(Image.open(f))
w, h = im.size
if max(w, h) > ms:
    im.thumbnail((ms, ms), Image.LANCZOS)
if out.lower().endswith(('.jpg', '.jpeg')):
    im = im.convert('RGB')
im.save(out, optimize=True, quality=q)
"
  "Pillow downscale + re-encode script.")

(defun my/org-image-file-size (file)
  "Return size of FILE in KB as a float."
  (/ (file-attribute-size (file-attributes file)) 1024.0))

(defun my/org-image--repoint-file-link (new-path)
  "Replace the [[file:...]] link at point with one pointing at NEW-PATH."
  (let ((ctx (org-element-context)))
    (unless (eq (org-element-type ctx) 'link)
      (user-error "Point is not on a link"))
    (let ((beg (org-element-property :begin ctx))
          (end (org-element-property :end ctx)))
      (delete-region beg end)
      (goto-char beg)
      (insert (format "[[file:%s]]" (org-link-escape new-path))))))

(defun my/org-image-optimize (&optional max-side quality update-link)
  "Re-encode the image at point to WebP (JPEG if the build lacks WebP).
MAX-SIDE caps the long edge (default 1920), QUALITY defaults to 80.  Writes a
NEW file next to the original and leaves the original untouched; repoints the
link if UPDATE-LINK is non-nil (interactively you get a y/n prompt).
With \\[universal-argument], prompt for MAX-SIDE and QUALITY."
  (interactive "P")
  (let* ((file (or (my/org-image-file-at-point) (user-error "No image file at point")))
         (max-side (or max-side (if current-prefix-arg
                                    (read-number "Max long edge (px): " 1920) 1920)))
         (quality (or quality (if current-prefix-arg
                                  (read-number "Quality (1-100): " 80) 80)))
         (ext (if (and (fboundp 'image-type-available-p) (image-type-available-p 'webp))
                  "webp" "jpg"))
         (new (expand-file-name (concat (file-name-sans-extension (file-name-nondirectory file)) "." ext)
                                (file-name-directory file)))
         (py (or (my/org-image-python)
                 (user-error "Need Python with Pillow: python3 -m pip install --user pillow")))
         (before (my/org-image-file-size file)))
    (unless (zerop (call-process py nil nil nil "-c" my/org-image-pil-optimize
                                 file new (number-to-string max-side) (number-to-string quality)))
      (user-error "Pillow failed on %s" file))
    (let ((after (my/org-image-file-size new)))
      (when (or update-link
                (y-or-n-p (format "%s -> %s (%.0fKB -> %.0fKB, -%.0f%%)  Repoint link?"
                                  (file-name-nondirectory file) (file-name-nondirectory new)
                                  before after
                                  (* 100 (/ (- before after) before)))))
        (my/org-image--repoint-file-link (file-relative-name new default-directory))
        (my/org-redisplay-images))
      (message "%s -> %s: %.0fKB -> %.0fKB (-%.0f%%)"
               (file-name-nondirectory file) (file-name-nondirectory new)
               before after (* 100 (/ (- before after) before))))))

(defun my/org-image-download-link-at-point ()
  "Materialize the remote http(s) image link at point into .images/ .
Downloads the image, rewrites the link to the local relative path and
redisplays inline images.  Failure leaves the original link untouched.
Deliberately a separate command rather than part of pasting: downloading is
synchronous and would block Emacs on every rich paste."
  (interactive)
  (require 'url-handlers)
  (let* ((ctx (org-element-context))
         (type (org-element-property :type ctx))
         (path (and ctx (org-element-property :path ctx))))
    (unless (member type '("http" "https"))
      (user-error "Point is not on an http(s) image link"))
    (let* ((base (car (split-string path "[?#]")))
           (ext  (downcase (or (file-name-extension base) "")))
           (img-dir (expand-file-name ".images" (file-name-directory buffer-file-name)))
           (dest (expand-file-name (file-name-nondirectory base) img-dir)))
      (unless (member ext '("png" "jpg" "jpeg" "gif" "webp" "svg" "bmp" "tiff"))
        (user-error "%s is not an image URL (extension %s)" base ext))
      (make-directory img-dir t)
      (condition-case nil
          (progn (url-copy-file (concat type ":" path) dest t)
                 (my/org-image--repoint-file-link (file-relative-name dest default-directory))
                 (my/org-redisplay-images)
                 (message "Downloaded %s" (file-name-nondirectory dest)))
        (error (user-error "Download failed — link left untouched"))))))
#+end_src

** Rotate image file permanently
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
;; 后端优先级: ImageMagick mogrify -> Python Pillow (见下个 block)
(defun my/org-rotate-image-file (&optional angle)
  "Rotate the image at point by ANGLE (default 90°) and redisplay.
Prefers ImageMagick `mogrify'; falls back to Python Pillow.  The file itself
is modified, so the rotation survives cache and reboot.
With \\[universal-argument], prompt for the angle."
  (interactive "P")
  (let* ((context (org-element-context))
         (type (org-element-property :type context))
         (path (when (member type '("file" "attachment"))
                 (org-element-property :path context)))
         ;; attachment: 的基准目录是 (org-attach-dir), 不是 org-attach-id-dir
         (file (when path (my/org-link-file-at-point)))
         (angle (cond ((numberp angle) angle)
                      ((not angle) 90)
                      (t (read-number "Rotation angle: " 90)))))
    (unless (and file (file-exists-p file) (file-regular-p file))
      (user-error "No image file at point"))
    (let* ((magick (my/org-image-magick))
           (py (unless magick (my/org-image-python)))
           (ok (cond
                (magick (zerop (shell-command
                                (format "\"%s\" mogrify -rotate %d \"%s\"" magick angle file))))
                (py (zerop (call-process py nil nil nil "-c" my/org-image-pil-rotate
                                         file (number-to-string angle)))))))
      (cond
       (ok (message "Rotated %s by %d°" (file-name-nondirectory file) angle)
           (my/org-redisplay-images))
       (magick (user-error "ImageMagick `mogrify` failed — check: magick mogrify -rotate 90 \"some.png\""))
       (py (user-error "Pillow rotation failed: %s" file))
       (t (user-error "No ImageMagick and no Python+Pillow — try: pacman -S imagemagick / apt install imagemagick"))))))
#+end_src

** DnD: 共享的 URI 解析与回退辅助（Org 已走内置 handler）
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(defun my/dnd--local-path (uri)
  "Return the local file path for a dropped URI, or nil.

`dnd-get-local-file-name' turns the XDND form \"file:///C:/foo\" into
\"/C:/foo\", which is NOT a valid path on MS-Windows.  Emacs 30+ rewrote
the w32 drag-and-drop backend and now emits that form, so drops silently
did nothing.  Strip the spurious leading slash(es) so both \"/C:/foo\" and
\"//C:/foo\" become \"C:/foo\".  UNC paths (//server/share/...) are left
alone because they have no drive letter."
  (let ((f (ignore-errors (dnd-get-local-file-name uri nil))))
    (when (and f (eq system-type 'windows-nt))
      (when (string-match "\\`//?\\([A-Za-z]:\\)" f)
        (setq f (replace-match "\\1" nil nil f))))
    f))

(defun my/dnd-fallback (uri action skip)
  "Hand URI back to the normal dnd dispatch, ignoring handler SKIP.

A dnd handler consumes its URI the moment it is called, even when it
returns nil -- so a handler that declines to act MUST re-dispatch, or the
drop silently does nothing.  SKIP is our own handler, excluded to avoid
re-entering ourselves."
  (let ((alist nil))
    (dolist (cell dnd-protocol-alist)
      (unless (eq (cdr cell) skip)
        (push cell alist)))
    (let ((dnd-protocol-alist (nreverse alist)))
      (dnd-handle-multiple-urls (selected-window) (list uri) action))))

;; ⚠️ 2026-10-13 删掉了 my/org-dnd-copy-to-data + my/org-prepend-flat-dnd-handler。
;; org-mode 自己在 (org-setup-yank-dnd-handlers) 里就装了 buffer-local 的
;; ^file: handler, 按 org-yank-dnd-method 走 —— 现在它是 'attach, 于是拖放
;; 直接进 org-attach-attach, 命中统一收口的 advice。原来那套是第三条管道,
;; 往扁平 data/ 写 [[file:]] 链接, 跟 org-attach 对不上。
#+end_src

** Delete file link in Markdown
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
;; 用 markdown-link-at-pos 解析, 同时处理 [file](pdf) 和 ![image](png)
(defun my/markdown-delete-file-link-at-point ()
  "Delete the file link at point and the underlying attached file."
  (interactive)
  (let ((info (markdown-link-at-pos (point))))
    (unless info (user-error "Not on a link"))
    (let* ((beg (nth 0 info)) (end (nth 1 info))
           (url (nth 3 info))
           (abspath (expand-file-name url default-directory))
           (result (my/delete-file-link--try-delete abspath)))
      (when result
        (delete-region beg end)
        (message (if (eq result 'deleted)
                     "Deleted file and removed link"
                   "Removed link (file was already missing"))))))
#+end_src

** DnD: Markdown
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(defconst my/markdown-image-extensions
  '("png" "jpg" "jpeg" "gif" "svg" "webp" "bmp" "avif")
  "Extensions treated as images for markdown DnD insertion.")

(defun my/markdown-dnd-copy-to-data (uri action)
  "Copy locally-dropped file URI into ~/org/data/ with denote-style name.
Prompts for keywords interactively.  Outside markdown-mode, or when
the URI is not a real local file, hand the drop back to normal dispatch."
  (let ((src (and (derived-mode-p 'markdown-mode) (my/dnd--local-path uri))))
    (if (and src (file-regular-p src))
        (let* ((keywords   (my/keywords-prompt))
               (dest-dir   (expand-file-name "data/" org-directory))
               (orig-name  (file-name-nondirectory src))
               (dest-name  (my/org-file-name-format orig-name keywords))
               (dest       (expand-file-name dest-name dest-dir))
               (ext        (downcase (or (file-name-extension orig-name) "")))
               (rel        (file-relative-name dest default-directory))
               (label      (file-name-base orig-name)))
          (make-directory dest-dir t)
          (copy-file src dest 1)
          (insert (if (member ext my/markdown-image-extensions)
                      (format "![%s](%s)" label rel)
                    (format "[%s](%s)" label rel)))
          'copy)
      (my/dnd-fallback uri action #'my/markdown-dnd-copy-to-data))))

(defun my/markdown-prepend-flat-dnd-handler ()
  "Prepend our flat-data DnD handler buffer-locally in markdown-mode."
  (setq-local dnd-protocol-alist
              (cons '("^file:" . my/markdown-dnd-copy-to-data)
                    dnd-protocol-alist)))
(add-hook 'markdown-mode-hook #'my/markdown-prepend-flat-dnd-handler)

;; Fallback registration on the DEFAULT value, not just per-buffer.
;; Some buffers (e.g. session-restored ones) never ran their mode hook, so
;; they never got the buffer-local handler above and fell through to
;; org-download-dnd -- which only handles images and, for anything else,
;; bails out to `dnd-open-local-file'.  That is why dropping an .epub opened
;; it in nov-mode instead of attaching it.  Both handlers decline cleanly via
;; `my/dnd-fallback' when the buffer is not theirs, so other modes are safe.
;; ⚠️ 2026-10-13 去掉了 org 那条: org-mode 现在走内置 handler + org-yank-dnd-method
;; = 'attach, 自己注册会抢在前面。markdown 那条保留。
(setq-default dnd-protocol-alist
              (append (list (cons "^file:" #'my/markdown-dnd-copy-to-data))
                      (default-value 'dnd-protocol-alist)))
#+end_src

* Org-gcal
** plstore workaround
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(after! plstore
  (advice-add 'plstore-save :around
              (lambda (orig-fun plstore)
                (let ((secret-alist (copy-tree (plstore--get-secret-alist plstore))))
                  (dolist (sec secret-alist)
                    (let ((pub (assoc (car sec) (plstore--get-alist plstore))))
                      (when pub (nconc pub (cdr sec)))))
                  (plstore--set-secret-alist plstore nil)
                  (unwind-protect
                      (funcall orig-fun plstore)
                    (plstore--set-secret-alist plstore secret-alist))))))
#+end_src

** Config & helpers
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package! org-gcal
  :commands (org-gcal-sync org-gcal-fetch org-gcal-delete-at-point org-gcal-post-at-point)
  :config
  (setq org-gcal-up-days 7
        org-gcal-down-days 60)

  (defvar my/org-gcal-default-calendar-id
    "f3f2ce4fb88adc5db8f25b71d3c75d20924a8c147a0feb34eafe477f173a860b@group.calendar.google.com")

  (defun my/org-gcal-drawer-timestamp ()
    "返回当前 entry 的 :org-gcal: drawer 里的时间戳。"
    (save-excursion
      (let ((end (save-excursion (outline-next-heading) (point))))
        (when (re-search-forward ":org-gcal:" end t)
          (let ((drawer-end (save-excursion
                              (re-search-forward ":END:" end t)
                              (point))))
            (let ((content (buffer-substring-no-properties (point) drawer-end)))
              (when (string-match "<[^>]+>" content)
                (match-string 0 content))))))))

  (defun my/org-gcal-set-drawer (timestamp)
    "把 TIMESTAMP 写入 :org-gcal: drawer。"
    (save-excursion
      (let* ((entry-start (point))
             (entry-end   (save-excursion (outline-next-heading) (point))))
        (goto-char entry-start)
        (if (re-search-forward "^:org-gcal:$" entry-end t)
            (let ((content-start (point)))
              (re-search-forward "^:END:$" entry-end t)
              (beginning-of-line)
              (delete-region content-start (point))
              (insert "\n" timestamp "\n"))
          (goto-char entry-start)
          (if (re-search-forward "^:END:$" entry-end t)
              (progn (end-of-line) (insert "\n:org-gcal:\n" timestamp "\n:END:"))
            (org-end-of-meta-data nil)
            (insert ":org-gcal:\n" timestamp "\n:END:\n"))))))

  (defun my/org-gcal-patch-status (calendar-id event-id gcal-status)
    "PATCH GCal event status."
    (require 'request)
    (let ((url (concat (org-gcal-events-url calendar-id)
                       "/" (url-hexify-string event-id)))
          (token (org-gcal--get-access-token calendar-id)))
      (request url
        :type "PATCH"
        :headers `(("Content-Type"  . "application/json")
                   ("Accept"        . "application/json")
                   ("Authorization" . ,(format "Bearer %s" token)))
        :data (json-encode `(("status" . ,gcal-status)))
        :parser 'org-gcal--json-read
        :success (cl-function
                  (lambda (&key _data &allow-other-keys)
                    (message "org-gcal: status → %s ✓ (%s)" gcal-status event-id)))
        :error (cl-function
                (lambda (&key error-thrown &allow-other-keys)
                  (message "org-gcal: PATCH status failed: %S" error-thrown))))))

  (defun my/org-gcal-todo-to-gcal-status (todo-state)
    (cond
     ((member todo-state '("DROPPED" "CANCELLED"))  "cancelled")
     ((member todo-state '("TODO" "NEXT" "WAITING" "HOLD" "DONE")) "confirmed")
     (t nil)))

  (defun my/org-gcal-auto-post ()
    "Auto push/update entries with timestamps to GCal."
    (interactive)
    (when (derived-mode-p 'org-mode)
      (org-save-outline-visibility t
        (org-map-entries
         (lambda ()
           (let* ((scheduled   (org-entry-get nil "SCHEDULED"))
                  (deadline    (org-entry-get nil "DEADLINE"))
                  (timestamp   (or scheduled deadline))
                  (has-id      (org-entry-get nil "entry-id"))
                  (calendar-id (or (org-entry-get nil "calendar-id")
                                   my/org-gcal-default-calendar-id))
                  (todo-state  (org-get-todo-state))
                  (gcal-status (my/org-gcal-todo-to-gcal-status todo-state))
                  (last-state  (org-entry-get nil "gcal-todo-state"))
                  (state-changed (and has-id gcal-status
                                      (not (equal last-state todo-state))))
                  (drawer-ts   (my/org-gcal-drawer-timestamp))
                  (ts-changed  (and timestamp has-id
                                    (or (not drawer-ts)
                                        (not (string= (string-trim timestamp)
                                                      (string-trim drawer-ts)))))))
             (when (and timestamp (or (not has-id) ts-changed))
               (my/org-gcal-set-drawer timestamp)
               (org-entry-put nil "calendar-id" calendar-id)
               (condition-case err
                   (org-gcal-post-at-point)
                 (error (message "org-gcal push failed: %s" err))))
             (when state-changed
               (let ((event-id (org-gcal--get-id (point))))
                 (when event-id
                   (org-entry-put nil "gcal-todo-state" todo-state)
                   (my/org-gcal-patch-status calendar-id event-id gcal-status))))))
         nil 'file))))

  ;; Dedup after fetch
  (defun my/org-gcal-dedup-after-fetch ()
    (let ((fetch-files (mapcar #'cdr org-gcal-fetch-file-alist))
          (known-ids (make-hash-table :test #'equal)))
      (dolist (file (org-agenda-files t))
        (unless (member (expand-file-name file) (mapcar #'expand-file-name fetch-files))
          (when (file-exists-p file)
            (with-temp-buffer
              (insert-file-contents file)
              (goto-char (point-min))
              (while (re-search-forward "^[ \t]*:entry-id:[ \t]+\\(.+\\)" nil t)
                (puthash (string-trim (match-string 1)) file known-ids))))))
      (dolist (fetch-file fetch-files)
        (let ((fpath (expand-file-name fetch-file)))
          (when (file-exists-p fpath)
            (with-current-buffer (find-file-noselect fpath)
              (org-with-wide-buffer
               (goto-char (point-min))
               (let ((kill-list nil))
                 (org-map-entries
                  (lambda ()
                    (let ((eid (org-entry-get nil "entry-id")))
                      (when (and eid (gethash eid known-ids))
                        (push (point) kill-list)))))
                 (when kill-list
                   (dolist (pos (sort kill-list #'>))
                     (goto-char pos)
                     (org-cut-subtree))
                   (save-buffer)
                   (message "org-gcal dedup: removed %d duplicate(s) from %s"
                            (length kill-list) fetch-file))))))))))

  (advice-add 'org-gcal-fetch :after
              (lambda (&rest _) (run-with-idle-timer 5 nil #'my/org-gcal-dedup-after-fetch)))

  ;; Periodic sync: idle timer 替代固定周期
  ;; 用户 idle 600s (10min) 后才触发, 避免后台静默偷网络/CPU
  ;; repeat 间隔 1800s, 但需要 idle 状态才会执行
  (run-with-idle-timer 600 t
                       (lambda ()
                         (when (not org-gcal--sync-lock)
                           (org-gcal-sync)
                           (my/org-gcal-auto-post)))))
#+end_src

* Knowledge Base

** org-roam
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
;; org-roam —— 2026-10-18 启用（笔记库从「7 个主文件 + subtree」迁向「原子节点 + db」）。
;; 安装 / 默认 UI / keybinds 由 Doom 的 `+roam' flag 负责（见 Bootstrap 的 init.el 块）；
;; 这里只放 ~/org 拓扑相关的口径，这些东西 Doom 不知道。
;;
;; 迁移阶段：P1a 已产出 ~/org/roam/{investment,device,health}/ 共 30 个节点
;; （脚本与校验在 ~/org/.agent/：migrate-p1.py 生成、verify-p1.py 独立复核）。
;; 源主文件 finance/things/health 暂时保留且仍是权威副本，二者并存到切换那一刻。
(after! org-roam
  (setq org-roam-directory (expand-file-name "roam/" org-directory) ; = ~/org/roam
        ;; db 放 Doom cache 而不是 ~/org：SQLite 每次 db-sync 都会变，
        ;; 放在被同步的目录里等于给 R2 / git 链路制造日常抖动。
        org-roam-db-location (expand-file-name "org-roam.db" doom-cache-dir)
        ;; 2026-10-19 起 dailies 正式启用: 日记从单文件 ~/org/journal.org
        ;; (日期是一级 heading) 拆成 ~/org/roam/daily/YYYY-MM-DD.org。
        ;;
        ;; ⚠️ 这里原来写过「dailies 不能用 + daily 被加密后 db 读密文失效」, 那是错的,
        ;; 2026-10-19 实测推翻: `org-roam-with-file' (org-roam-utils.el:99) 只把
        ;; `find-file-hook' / `auto-mode-alist' 置 nil, 而 epa 是靠
        ;; `file-name-handler-alist' 接管 `insert-file-contents' —— **索引时照样解密**,
        ;; buffer 里是明文。实测 21 个加密 daily 文件 force sync 全部正常建 node。
        ;; 唯一的前提是路径要匹配 `epa-file-name-regexp' (已加 roam[\\/]daily 分支),
        ;; 否则 dailies 建出来的文件是**明文**。
        org-roam-dailies-directory (expand-file-name "daily/" org-roam-directory))

  ;; dailies capture 模板。默认模板的 head 只有 `#+title:', **不带 :ID:** ——
  ;; 而没有 ID 的文件不进 nodes 表 (实测 nodes=0), 搜不到也连不上反链。
  ;; 硬约束: `:PROPERTIES:' 抽屉必须在 `#+title:' **之前**, 否则 roam 解析错。
  ;; 不写 `#+filetags:' —— daily/ 目录本身已经表达了「这是日记」, 再加一层 tag
  ;; 只是给 roam 的 tags 表添一个天天重复的维度。
  (setq org-roam-dailies-capture-templates
        '(("d" "default" entry
           "* %<%H:%M>\n%?"
           :target (file+head "%<%Y-%m-%d>.org"
                              ":PROPERTIES:\n:ID:       %(org-id-new)\n:END:\n#+title: %<%Y-%m-%d %a>\n")
           :empty-lines 1)))

  ;; 目录维度的过滤前缀让位给元标记（2026-10-19）。
  ;; Doom 的 `org-roam-node-doom-type' 字段**本身就是目录** —— 它是
  ;; `file-relative-name' + `directory-file-name' 算出来的「相对 roam 的路径」
  ;; (lang/org/autoload/contrib-roam.el:59-80), 默认前缀是 `@'
  ;; (lang/org/contrib/roam.el:76)。而同一次整理把元标记统一成了
  ;; `@index @shorts @memo @idea @nsfw @skip @downloaded', 两者会撞:
  ;; 敲 `@index' 时既匹配「目录名含 index 的节点」又匹配「打了 @index 的节点」。
  ;; 解法是把目录的前缀改成 `d:' —— node-find 里 `d:emacs' 过滤目录、
  ;; `@index' 过滤角色, 两套语义各走各的。
  (setq org-roam-node-template-prefixes
        (cons '("doom-type" . "d:")
              (cl-remove-if (lambda (p) (equal (car p) "doom-type"))
                            org-roam-node-template-prefixes)))

  ;; ---- 新建节点必须带 :ID: (2026-10-21) --------------------------------
  ;; 默认模板的 head 只有 `#+title:' 一行, 而 org-roam-capture 只在**内存里的 node
  ;; 对象**上补 ID (org-roam-capture.el:404-405 `(or ... (org-id-new))'), **不落盘**
  ;; —— head 里不写, 文件里就没有 :ID: → 该文件不进 nodes 表, node-find 搜不到、
  ;; 反链连不上, 而且**不报错** (dailies 那条实测 nodes=0 是同一个坑)。
  ;; 硬约束: `:PROPERTIES:' 抽屉必须在 `#+title:' **之前**, 否则 roam 解析错。
  ;; 文件名模板与 org-roam 默认值一致 (`%<%Y%m%d%H%M%S>-${slug}.org'), 显式写出来
  ;; 只为和 head 放一起看。`%(org-id-new)' 的产出形态由 org-id-method 决定 ——
  ;; 本机已是 'ts + "%Y%m%dT%H%M%S", 与存量节点的「文件名时间戳」ID 口径一致。
  ;; 不写 `#+filetags:': 领域 tag 由人决定, 建完自己加。
  (setq org-roam-capture-templates
        '(("d" "default" plain "%?"
           :target (file+head "%<%Y%m%d%H%M%S>-${slug}.org"
                              ":PROPERTIES:\n:ID:       %(org-id-new)\n:END:\n#+title: ${title}\n")
           :unnarrowed t)))

  ;; ---- heading → 独立节点: 给 org-roam-extract-subtree 补落点 (2026-10-21) ----
  ;; 原生实现 (org-roam-node.el:990-1029) 最后一步是
  ;;   (read-file-name "Extract node to:" (file-name-as-directory org-roam-directory)
  ;;                  template nil template)
  ;; dir 参数写死是 roam **根**, 预填名只有 `20261021T003635-slug.org' —— 直接回车
  ;; 就落 `~/org/roam/xxx.org', 不属于任何领域: node-find 里显示成 `d:roam' 过滤不到。
  ;; 库里那 3 个根直属文件就是这么来的。
  ;; 解法: 先选一级领域目录, let-bind `org-roam-extract-new-file-path' 把目录名拼进
  ;; 预填值 —— 它只是 minibuffer 的 default/initial, 不进全局变量; 剪子树 / promote /
  ;; 写 ID 那些逻辑一行没重写, 全走原生 `org-roam-extract-subtree'。
  ;; 目录列表排除 `daily/': 日记不是抽取目的地。
  (defun my/org-roam-extract-to-domain ()
    "抽取当前 subtree 成 roam 节点, 落点先选 ~/org/roam 下的一级领域目录。"
    (interactive)
    (let* ((dirs (seq-filter
                  (lambda (f)
                    (and (file-directory-p f)
                         (not (equal (file-relative-name f org-roam-directory) "daily"))))
                  (directory-files org-roam-directory t "^[^.]")))
           (dir (completing-read
                 "领域目录: "
                 (mapcar (lambda (f) (file-relative-name f org-roam-directory)) dirs)
                 nil t)))
      (let ((org-roam-extract-new-file-path
             (concat dir "/%<%Y%m%d%H%M%S>-${slug}.org")))
        (call-interactively #'org-roam-extract-subtree))))

  ;; ---- 刻意不做的两件事（要改之前先想清楚）----
  ;; 1. 不要把 org-roam-directory 塞进 org-agenda-files：roam 里的 TODO 绝大多数是
  ;;    记录条目的伪状态（media 628 + read 234 条的"看过/读过"），整目录进 agenda
  ;;    会瞬间毁掉任务视图。agenda 保持显式列表不变。
  ;;    2026-10-18 退役后已解: refile 现在显式指向 roam/ (见 my/org-roam-node-files
  ;;    与 my/org-roam-bulk-files), roam 是唯一写入目的地。
  ;; 2. 附件池**仍**维持 ~/org/data/ 平铺: 当初卡在这里的前提 (roam 节点与源主文件
  ;;    会对同一张图指向两个位置) 随退役消失了, per-node 化技术上随时可做。
  ;;    没做的理由是性价比 —— 见 .agent/p3-retire-report.org。要点: 全池 137 个 /
  ;;    536MB, 且 20260425T000524--染色体检查报告.pdf 被两个 roam 节点同时引用,
  ;;    per-node 下一条必然断。而收益只是「org-attach 知道图属于哪个 heading」,
  ;;    这个库本来就得靠扫 subtree 链接回答该问题。真要切, 先解掉那个共享附件
  ;;    (复制成两份, 或那条改用 file: 硬路径)。
  )
#+end_src

** markdown-ts-appear
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
;; markdown-ts-appear —— markdown-ts-mode 的渲染增强: "Rendered while reading. Source when editing."
;; 依赖: Emacs 31 内置 markdown-ts-mode (本机 31.1, API 已验证兼容) + markdown-table-wrap
;; 生效范围: 仅 markdown-ts-mode 缓冲区。.md 默认仍走 Doom 的 markdown-mode
;; (DnD handler 等既有 hook 不动), 需要时 M-x markdown-ts-mode 手动切换。
;; 是否让 .md 默认用 markdown-ts-mode 打开, 等 Sean 拍板, 先不动 auto-mode-alist。
(use-package! markdown-ts-appear
  :hook (markdown-ts-mode . markdown-ts-appear-mode))

;; Evil 集成 (Sean 用 evil +everywhere): 只在插入态显示源码, normal 态保持渲染视图。
;; 这是 README 为 evil 用户推荐的用法; 删掉本段即恢复上游默认 (point 进元素即显源码)。
(defun my/markdown-appear-evil ()
  "Reveal Markdown source only in Evil insert state."
  (if markdown-ts-appear-mode
      (progn
        (add-hook 'evil-insert-state-entry-hook #'markdown-ts-appear-start nil t)
        (add-hook 'evil-insert-state-exit-hook #'markdown-ts-appear-stop nil t)
        (unless (eq (bound-and-true-p evil-state) 'insert)
          (markdown-ts-appear-stop)))
    (remove-hook 'evil-insert-state-entry-hook #'markdown-ts-appear-start t)
    (remove-hook 'evil-insert-state-exit-hook #'markdown-ts-appear-stop t)))

(add-hook 'markdown-ts-appear-mode-hook #'my/markdown-appear-evil)

;; 可选视觉/预览选项 (注释掉的即上游默认值, 按需取消注释):
;; (setopt markdown-ts-appear-heading-bullets '("◉" "○" "▸" "•"))  ; ATX 标题按层级循环符号
;; (setopt markdown-ts-appear-link-icon "🔗")                      ; 普通链接前图标
;; (setopt markdown-ts-appear-code-fence-style 'connected)         ; 带语言标签的连通围栏
;; (setopt markdown-ts-appear-table-style 'wrapped)                ; 宽度感知表格 (默认值)
;; 数学预览: 需 MELPA mathjax 包 + node (本机 v26.8.2 ✓) + Emacs SVG 支持:
;; (setopt markdown-ts-appear-enable-math-preview t)
;; Mermaid 预览: 需 mmdc (npm i -g @mermaid-js/mermaid-cli) + Chrome, 本机未装:
;; (setopt markdown-ts-appear-enable-mermaid-preview t)
#+end_src

** consult-notes
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
;; 2026-10-17: denote 已卸载, 原来按文件名时间戳排序 denote 文件的
;; `my/consult-notes-files-newest-first' 一并删除 —— 笔记库只有根目录 7 个
;; 主文件, 搜索入口是下面这个 org-headings 源。
(use-package! consult-notes
  :after (consult org)
  :commands (consult-notes consult-notes-search-in-all-notes)
  :config
  ;; 2026-10-18 退役: 六个根主文件已并入 ~/org/roam/。改成递归收 roam 全部
  ;; 节点 + 根目录残存的 inbox/journal —— 这样新增 roam 节点不用回来改表。
  ;; journal.org 是加密的, consult-notes 读不进去 (只会被当成乱码),
  ;; 和 agenda / 孤儿扫描一样不列入。
  ;; 2026-10-21 五改: **排除 roam/daily/** (用 my/org-daily-file-p 而不是内联正则,
  ;;   这个块在 2538 行之后求值, 谓词已经定义好了)。理由两条:
  ;;   1. daily 是 GPG 密文, `consult-notes--org-headings' 用 org-map-entries
  ;;      逐个打开读 -> 236 个文件全是二进制, 候选区一堆乱码;
  ;;   2. `consult-notes-search-in-all-notes' 的 ripgrep 目录**就是**这个变量
  ;;      (见 consult-notes.el:321), 所以这里排掉了 = 全文搜索也一并排掉。
  ;;      日记的跨天全文检索走 `my/journal-search' (SPC n n J), 它才认密文。
  ;;   注意: 只排 daily, **保留 media/ read/** —— 搜书名影名是刚需。
  (setq consult-notes-org-headings-files
        (append (list (expand-file-name "inbox.org" org-directory))
                (seq-remove #'my/org-daily-file-p
                            (directory-files-recursively
                             (expand-file-name "roam/" org-directory) "\\.org\\'"))))
  (consult-notes-org-headings-mode))

(after! consult-notes
  (consult-customize consult-notes :sort nil)
  ;; 文件名筛选不区分大小写
  (setq completion-ignore-case t)
  ;; 全文搜索不区分大小写 (ripgrep)
  (setq consult-ripgrep-args
        "rg --null --line-buffered --color=never --max-columns=1000 --path-separator / --no-heading --with-filename --line-number --search-zip --ignore-case"))
#+end_src

(use-package! org-transclusion
  :after org
  :commands (org-transclusion-add
             org-transclusion-add-all

* Writing
** jinx (spell-check)
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
;; jinx: 基于 enchant 的高速增量拼写检查
;; - 比 flyspell 快一个数量级, 只检查可见区域
;; - Windows/MSYS2 依赖: pkg-config/pkgconf 能找到 enchant-2
;;   也就是 `pkg-config --exists enchant-2` 返回 0
;; - 首次使用: M-x jinx-languages 选择字典 (如 en_US)
;; - 在错词上 M-$ 显示候选
;; - 默认不在 org-mode 开启 (中英混排误报较多), 想开启在 hook 里加上
;; - Windows 上 jinx-mod.dll + enchant 已触发过 Emacs native crash,
;;   所以这里不在 Windows 自动启用。
(use-package! jinx
  :if (not (featurep :system 'windows))
  :hook ((markdown-mode . jinx-mode))
  :bind (("M-$"   . jinx-correct)
         ("C-M-$" . jinx-languages))
  :config
  ;; Linux 侧系统只装了 en_US(见 /usr/share/hunspell/), 不锁死语言的话
  ;; jinx 会照 locale 去找 zh_CN 并反复报 "No dictionaries available"。
  ;; 中英混排场景下固定英文词典, 只检查英文单词。
  (setq jinx-languages "en_US"))
#+end_src

** Focus mode toggle
一键进入专注写作：居中宽栏 + 隐藏 modeline + 图注自动编号。再按一次还原。

和 ~SPC t z~（纯 writeroom）不冲突：这里额外隐藏 modeline 并把栏宽临时调到 110，
适合写 journal / 长文时用。

#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(defvar my/focus-writing-active nil)
(defvar my/focus-writing--saved-width nil)

(defun my/focus-writing-toggle ()
  "Toggle distraction-free writing layout (writeroom + no modeline)."
  (interactive)
  (require 'writeroom-mode)
  (if my/focus-writing-active
      (progn
        (when (fboundp 'hide-mode-line-mode) (hide-mode-line-mode -1))
        (when writeroom-mode (writeroom-mode -1))
        (when my/focus-writing--saved-width
          (setq writeroom-width my/focus-writing--saved-width))
        (setq my/focus-writing-active nil)
        (message "Focus mode: OFF"))
    (setq my/focus-writing--saved-width writeroom-width
          writeroom-width 110)
    (writeroom-mode 1)
    (when (fboundp 'hide-mode-line-mode) (hide-mode-line-mode 1))
    (setq my/focus-writing-active t)
    (message "Focus mode: ON (writeroom 110, modeline hidden)")))
#+end_src

* Journal
** Journal encryption
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(use-package! epa-file
  :demand t
  :config
  ;; PortableGit's bundled gnupg has no keyboxd/gpg-preset-passphrase, so point
  ;; EPG at the full Git-for-Windows gnupg instead (same ~/.gnupg homedir).
  (when (file-executable-p "c:/Users/yuan_/scoop/apps/git/current/usr/bin/gpg.exe")
    (setq epg-gpg-program "c:/Users/yuan_/scoop/apps/git/current/usr/bin/gpg.exe"))
  ;; Journal 文件名保持 .org（journal.org），只加密磁盘上的文件内容。
  ;;
  ;; 这里走的是**公钥加密**（epa-file-encrypt-to指定了公钥 ID），所以
  ;; `epa-file-cache-passphrase-for-symmetric-encryption' 和
  ;; `password-cache-expiry' 这类"记住口令"的设置对它一概无效 ——
  ;; 那两个只作用于 org-crypt-symmetric t 的对称加密路径。曾在这里配置过
  ;; 它们却依然每次被问密码，就是这个原因，故已移除。
  ;;
  ;; 现在改为让密钥本身不设口令（加密子密钥 cv25519 43A3B8E05F342057
  ;; 已去除保护），解密因此无需口令，Emacs 也就不会再弹 pinentry。
  ;; 备份见 ~/.gnupg.backup-20261011-205326.tar.gz。
  ;; 2026-10-16 二次改: 日记文件当时落在 ~/org/journal.org (与 inbox.org 同级),
  ;; 不再位于 journal/ 目录下 —— 旧正则按目录匹配, 会漏掉它 → 明文落盘。
  ;; 2026-10-19 三次改: journal 拆成 org-roam-dailies (一天一文件), 现在是「三选一」:
  ;;   1) ~/org/journal/**/*.org         journal/ 下的任何 .org (归档 + 备份)
  ;;   2) ~/org/roam/daily/*.org         每天的日记 (新形态)
  ;;   3) ~/org/journal.org              根目录的单文件日记 (历史形态,
  ;;                                     2026-10-19 拆完已移进 journal/_orig-20261019/)
  ;; 第 1 条不能省: _orig-20261016/ 里的旧日记同样是密文,
  ;; 不匹配就打不开 (只看到乱码)。
  ;; 注意别写成 ~.*\.org~: 那会把 notes/ 下的明文笔记也当成加密文件, 保存时
  ;; 全库笔记都会被加密。
  ;;
  ;; 2026-10-19 加第三条 `roam[\\/]daily': journal 已拆成 org-roam-dailies 的
  ;; 「一天一文件」(~/org/roam/daily/YYYY-MM-DD.org)。少了这一条，新建的日记
  ;; 会明文落盘 —— 这是本次迁移唯一不可逆的风险点。
  ;; 仍然**不能**放宽成按内容/按目录任意匹配，见上面 ~.*\.org~ 那条。
  (setq epa-file-name-regexp
        ".*[\\\\/]\\(journal\\|roam[\\\\/]daily\\)\\([\\\\/].*\\)?\\.org\\'"
        epa-file-encrypt-to "CE8EA11D470516F3220E9BA11EE5F16F26111AD1"
        epa-file-select-keys 'silent)
  (epa-file-name-regexp-update)
  (epa-file-enable))
#+end_src

** 明文兜底：非 PGP 数据的日记文件也能打开
~epa-file-name-regexp~ 把 journal/ 下所有 ~__journal.org~ 一律当成加密文件，
只要文件名匹配就无条件走 ~epg-decrypt-file~。于是任何在 Emacs 之外写出来的
明文文件（Trilium 同步、git pull、备份还原、新建未保存）一打开就报
~Can't decrypt, No data (no armored data)~，连内容都看不到，更没法修。
这里在读取前先看一眼文件头：确认不是 OpenPGP 数据就退回普通插入。
明文打开后正常保存，写盘时仍会按名字加密，文件自动回到加密态。
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(defun my/file-pgp-data-p (file)
  "Return non-nil if FILE starts with OpenPGP data (armored or binary)."
  (and (file-readable-p file)
       (> (file-attribute-size (file-attributes file)) 0)
       (with-temp-buffer
         (set-buffer-multibyte nil)
         (insert-file-contents-literally file nil 0 300)
         (let ((head (buffer-string)))
           (or (string-match-p "-----BEGIN PGP" head)
               ;; 二进制 OpenPGP 旧格式 CTB：bit7 置位、bit6 清零。
               ;; UTF-8 文本首字节不会落在 0x80-0xBF，判断是安全的。
               (= (logand (aref head 0) #xC0) #x80))))))

(defun my/epa-file-insert-file-contents-advice (orig file &optional visit beg end replace)
  "Insert FILE as plain text when it holds no OpenPGP data.
Otherwise defer to `epa-file-insert-file-contents'."
  (if (and (file-exists-p file)
           (not (my/file-pgp-data-p file)))
      (epa-file-run-real-handler #'insert-file-contents
                                 (list file visit beg end replace))
    (funcall orig file visit beg end replace)))

(with-eval-after-load 'epa-file
  ;; `epa-file-run-real-handler' 是宏，必须在本文件加载后才展开。
  (advice-add 'epa-file-insert-file-contents :around
              #'my/epa-file-insert-file-contents-advice))
#+end_src

** journal（单文件定位与 capture）
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
;; calendar 里 f / n / F / N 跳到对应日期的日记 heading。
;; 原来挂在 denote-journal 的 :init 里, 2026-10-17 卸载 denote-journal 后独立出来。
;; (denote-journal 的「一天一文件」假设在单文件 journal.org 下根本不成立。)
(with-eval-after-load 'calendar
  (define-key calendar-mode-map (kbd "f") #'my/journal-calendar-goto)
  (define-key calendar-mode-map (kbd "n") #'my/journal-calendar-goto)
  (with-eval-after-load 'evil
    (evil-define-key '(normal motion emacs) calendar-mode-map
      (kbd "F") #'my/journal-calendar-goto
      (kbd "N") #'my/journal-calendar-goto)))

;;; --- journal 定位 (2026-10-19 起: org-roam-dailies 一天一文件) --------
;; 日记形态第三次变更:
;;   一天一文件 (denote) → 一年一文件 → 单文件 journal.org (2026-10-16)
;;   → ~/org/roam/daily/YYYY-MM-DD.org (2026-10-19, roam 节点 + GPG 加密)
;;
;; 回到「一天一文件」不是绕圈 —— 这次每天的文件是 roam 节点 (db / 双链 / 反链),
;; 而且 10-16 的冲突实证支持这个形状: .stversions 里 5 个冲突全是共写单文件的
;; append-note, journal 是 0 —— 一天一文件天然不冲突。
;;
;; 加密不再靠文件名, 靠 `epa-file-name-regexp' 的 roam[\\/]daily 分支 ——
;; 读写都经 epa handler, 磁盘上是密文, buffer 里是明文。
;; 日期 heading 取消了: 文件名就是日期, 原 `** HH:MM' 整体上移成 `* HH:MM'。

(defun my/journal--effective-time ()
  "当前时间。03:00 前算前一天 (熬夜写日记不跨到新的一天)。"
  (let ((now (decode-time)))
    (if (< (nth 2 now) 3)
        (time-subtract (current-time) (seconds-to-time 86400))
      (current-time))))

(defun my/journal-file-for (&optional time)
  "TIME (默认今天) 那天日记的文件路径, 形如 roam/daily/2026-10-19.org。"
  (expand-file-name (format-time-string "%Y-%m-%d.org" (or time (my/journal--effective-time)))
                    (or (and (boundp 'org-roam-dailies-directory) org-roam-dailies-directory)
                        (expand-file-name "roam/daily/" org-directory))))

(defun my/journal-date-heading (time)
  "TIME 对应的日期文本, 形如 `2026-10-16 Fri'。"
  (format-time-string "%Y-%m-%d %a" time))

(defun my/journal--ensure-file-header (file time)
  "确保 daily 文件 FILE 有 roam 要求的头。文件不存在时才写, 返回是否新建。

必须在这里写而不是指望 capture 模板: 模板 j 走的是**普通** org-capture,
不经过 org-roam 的头注入。少了 `:ID:', 这个文件就不进 nodes 表
(2026-10-19 实测: 没 ID 的文件 nodes=0), 搜不到、也连不上反链。

硬约束: `:PROPERTIES:' 抽屉必须在 `#+title:' 之前。
写入走 `write-region' → epa handler, 落盘自动是密文。"
  (let ((new (not (file-exists-p file))))
    (when new
      (make-directory (file-name-directory file) t)
      (with-temp-file file
        (insert (format ":PROPERTIES:\n:ID:       %s\n:END:\n#+title: %s\n\n"
                        (org-id-new) (my/journal-date-heading time)))))
    new))

(defun my/journal-goto-time (time &optional create)
  "打开 TIME 那天日记。CREATE 非 nil 时文件不存在也打开。"
  (let ((file (my/journal-file-for time)))
    (when (or (file-exists-p file) create)
      (my/journal--ensure-file-header file time)
      (find-file file)
      t)))

(defun my/journal-capture-target ()
  "org-capture 用: 定位到今天日记文件的末尾。

返回 point (org-capture function target 约定)。"
  (let* ((time (my/journal--effective-time))
         (file (my/journal-file-for time)))
    (my/journal--ensure-file-header file time)
    (set-buffer (org-capture-target-buffer file))
    (goto-char (point-max))
    ;; 文件末尾没有换行时, 直接 insert 会把 `* HH:MM' 接到上一行屁股上
    (unless (bolp) (insert "\n"))
    (point)))

(defun my/journal-calendar-goto ()
  "从 calendar 光标处的日期跳到那天的日记。"
  (interactive)
  (let* ((d (calendar-cursor-to-date t))   ; (month day year)
         (time (encode-time 0 0 0 (nth 1 d) (nth 0 d) (nth 2 d)))
         (file (my/journal-file-for time)))
    (if (file-exists-p file)
        (find-file file)
      (message "这天没有日记: %s" (my/journal-date-heading time)))))
#+end_src

** Weather (wttr.in) — async
#+begin_src emacs-lisp
(defvar my/weather-location "上海"
  "wttr.in 查询地点。")

(defvar my/weather--cache nil
  "(日期 . 天气字符串) 缓存, 同一天不再重复请求 wttr.in。")

(defun my/weather--url ()
  "返回 wttr.in 单行天气的 URL。"
  (format "https://wttr.in/%s?format=3"
          (url-hexify-string my/weather-location)))

(defun my/get-weather ()
  "同步获取天气 (手动调用 `my/insert-weather' 时用)。"
  (let ((coding-system-for-read 'utf-8))
    (string-trim
     (shell-command-to-string
      (format "curl -s --connect-timeout 5 --max-time 10 \"%s\""
              (my/weather--url))))))

(defun my/insert-weather ()
  "在光标处插入当前天气 (同步, 可能卡 ≤10s)。"
  (interactive)
  (insert (my/get-weather)))

(defun my/weather--fetch (callback &optional attempt)
  "异步取天气, 非空结果交给 CALLBACK。空结果或 curl 报错时重试一次。

超时放宽到 10s: wttr.in 在国内经常 3s 拿不到, 旧版 --max-time 3
是大部分日记没有天气的直接原因。"
  (let* ((attempt (or attempt 1))
         (buf (generate-new-buffer " *weather*")))
    (make-process
     :name "weather-fetch"
     :buffer buf
     :command (list "curl" "-sS" "--connect-timeout" "5" "--max-time" "10"
                    (my/weather--url))
     :sentinel
     (lambda (proc _event)
       (when (memq (process-status proc) '(exit signal))
         (let ((output (with-current-buffer buf
                         (string-trim (buffer-string)))))
           (kill-buffer buf)
           (cond
            ;; curl 失败时 -sS 会把错误写进 stdout, 别当成天气写进日记。
            ((or (string-empty-p output)
                 (string-prefix-p "curl" output))
             (when (< attempt 2)
               (my/weather--fetch callback (1+ attempt))))
            (t
             (setq my/weather--cache
                   (cons (format-time-string "%Y-%m-%d") output))
             (funcall callback output)))))))))

(defun my/weather--get (callback)
  "取天气: 当天缓存命中就直接回调, 否则发起异步请求。"
  (let ((today (format-time-string "%Y-%m-%d")))
    (if (and my/weather--cache
             (string= (car my/weather--cache) today))
        (funcall callback (cdr my/weather--cache))
      (my/weather--fetch callback))))

(defun my/journal--today-weather-p (&optional buffer)
  "今天那篇日记 (一个 daily 文件) 里是否已经有天气条目。

2026-10-19 改: 一个文件 = 一天, 于是可以直接全文件搜 —— 不再需要先算
「今天 heading 的边界」。旧的 `my/journal--today-bounds' / `my/journal--today-heading-re'
是单文件模型的产物 (要在整个文件里圈出今天那一区), 已删。
连带修掉的旧 bug: 过去整个文件里任何一条天气都会命中, 于是「今天」
永远被认为已经有天气。"
  (with-current-buffer (or buffer (current-buffer))
    (save-excursion
      (save-restriction
        (widen)
        (goto-char (point-min))
        (re-search-forward "^\\*+[ \t]+天气[ \t]*$" nil t)))))

(defun my/journal--insert-today-weather (buffer text)
  "在 BUFFER 里插入 `* 天气' + TEXT。已经有了就不插, 返回是否插入。

插入点是第一个 heading 之前 —— 天气是当天最上面一条。层级从 `**' 降为 `*':
2026-10-19 起日期 heading 取消, 当天条目整体上移一级。
不在这里跳空行: 刚建好的日记文件往往末尾跟着一串空行, 跳过去等于把
天气插到空行之后, 而且那个 `while (looking-at-p '^[ \t]*$')' 在文件
末尾是个死循环 (旧代码就栽在这, Emacs 吃满一个核)。"
  (with-current-buffer buffer
    (save-excursion
      (save-restriction
        (widen)
        (let (inserted)
          (when (not (my/journal--today-weather-p buffer))
            (goto-char (point-min))
            (if (re-search-forward "^\\*+ " nil t)
                (goto-char (match-beginning 0))   ; 第一个 heading 的行首
              (goto-char (point-max)))
            (insert "* 天气\n" text "\n\n")
            (setq inserted t))
          inserted)))))

(defun my/journal--live-buffer ()
  "日记文件的 base buffer —— 在内存里是明文, 可以直接改。没打开返回 nil。

取 base buffer 是为了绕开 org-capture 的 indirect buffer: 它跟 base 共享
文本但有自己的 narrowing, 在它里面 `insert' 会插进 capture 正在编辑的内容。"
  (let ((buf (get-file-buffer (my/journal-file-for))))
    (when (and buf (buffer-live-p buf))
      (or (buffer-base-buffer buf) buf))))

(defun my/journal--file-p (&optional file)
  "FILE (默认当前 buffer 的文件) 是不是日记文件。"
  (let ((file (or file (buffer-file-name))))
    (and file
         (string= (expand-file-name file)
                  (expand-file-name (my/journal-file-for))))))

(defun my/journal-ensure-today-weather (&rest _args)
  "今天那条日记还没有天气时, 异步补一条。

2026-10-16 日记合并进单个 `journal.org' 之后, 旧逻辑三条路全断:
1. `denote-after-new-note-hook' 里判断文件名含 `__journal' —— 不再有按天
   denote 文件, 永远不匹配;
2. 旧 `my/journal--insert-weather' 在整个文件里搜 `^*+ 天气' —— 234 条
   旧日记里任何一条天气都会命中, 于是「今天」永远被认为已经有天气;
3. 插到文件顶部 —— 单文件下那是 2026-03 那条日记的头, 不是今天。

现在改成: 先定位今天的日期 heading, 只在那条日记的范围内判断和插入。

只在日记 buffer 已经在内存里 (明文) 时动手, 绝不在这里
`find-file-noselect' 加密文件 —— 异步哨兵里解密会弹口令框把 Emacs 卡住。
buffer 没开就不补, 下一条 hook 会兜住。"
  (let ((buffer (my/journal--live-buffer)))
    (when (and buffer
               (not (my/journal--today-weather-p buffer)))
      (my/weather--get
       (lambda (text)
         (when (and (buffer-live-p buffer)
                    (my/journal--insert-today-weather buffer text))
           (with-current-buffer buffer
             (save-buffer))
           (message "已补当天天气: %s" text))))
      t)))

(defun my/journal-insert-weather ()
  "手动给今天的日记补一条天气 (自动补失败 / 网络挂了时用)。"
  (interactive)
  (unless (my/journal--live-buffer)
    (find-file (my/journal-file-for)))
  (if (my/journal--today-weather-p)
      (message "今天的日记已经有天气了")
    (my/journal-ensure-today-weather)
    (message "正在取天气…")))

;; 手动打开日记 / calendar 跳转 / capture 定位目标文件, 都必经 find-file,
;; 挂这里覆盖大部分场景。
(add-hook 'find-file-hook #'my/journal-ensure-today-weather)

;; 写日记的主入口其实是 org-capture (`SPC n n j' → 模板 j)。capture 期间
;; 目标文件是 narrowed 的 indirect buffer, 在那里插入会污染正在编辑的
;; 内容 —— 所以等 finalize 之后 (当天的文件已写好并落盘) 再补。
(add-hook 'org-capture-after-finalize-hook #'my/journal-ensure-today-weather)

(defun my/journal-today ()
  "打开今天的日记 (文件不存在就建)。"
  (interactive)
  (my/journal-goto-time (my/journal--effective-time) t))

;;; --- 跨天全文检索 (dailies 缺的能力, 自己补) -------------------------
;; 「一天一文件 + 全文件加密」拆掉了旧的全文检索: org-roam 的 db 不索引正文
;; (2026-10-19 实测), 而 `rg' / `grep' 读的是磁盘上的密文。org-journal 有
;; 现成的 `j s y', dailies 什么都没有 —— 这是选 dailies 唯一实打实的代价。
;;
;; 补法是遍历 roam/daily/, 用 `insert-file-contents' 逐个读: 它走
;; `file-name-handler-alist' 里的 epa handler, 读进来的就是明文。
;; 明文只存在于临时 buffer, 从不落盘、也不建 buffer 访问文件
;; (所以不会触发 find-file-hook, 不会误插天气)。
;;
;; 代价: 236 个文件逐个解密, 实测约 40s。用 `C-u' 前缀可只搜最近 90 天。
;; gpg-agent 缓存过期时中途会弹口令框 —— 这是加密日记的固有成本。

(defvar my/journal-search-history nil)

(defun my/journal--daily-files (&optional since-days)
  "roam/daily/ 下的日记文件。SINCE-DAYS 非 nil 时只要最近这些天的。"
  (let* ((dir (or (and (boundp 'org-roam-dailies-directory)
                       org-roam-dailies-directory)
                  (expand-file-name "roam/daily/" org-directory)))
         (files (directory-files dir t "\\.org\\'")))
    (if since-days
        (let ((cut (format-time-string
                    "%Y-%m-%d"
                    (time-subtract (current-time)
                                   (seconds-to-time (* 86400 since-days))))))
          (cl-remove-if (lambda (f) (string< (file-name-base f) cut))
                        files))
      files)))

(defun my/journal-search--collect (query since-days)
  "搜 QUERY, 返回 ((文件 行号) . 显示串) 列表。QUERY 是正则。"
  (let* ((files (sort (my/journal--daily-files since-days) #'string<))
         (n (length files))
         (i 0)
         (out nil))
    (dolist (f files)
      (setq i (1+ i))
      (message "搜日记 %d/%d %s…" i n (file-name-base f))
      (with-temp-buffer
        (ignore-errors (insert-file-contents f))
        (goto-char (point-min))
        (while (re-search-forward query nil t)
          (let ((line (line-number-at-pos))
                (text (string-trim
                       (buffer-substring-no-properties
                        (line-beginning-position) (line-end-position)))))
            (push (cons (list f line)
                        (format "%s:%d: %s" (file-name-base f) line text))
                  out)))))
    (nreverse out)))

(defun my/journal-search (query &optional since-days)
  "在所有日记里搜 QUERY (正则), 选中一条直接跳过去。

SINCE-DAYS 限制只搜最近这些天。交互下 `C-u' 前缀 = 最近 90 天。"
  (interactive
   (list (read-regexp "搜日记 (regexp)" nil 'my/journal-search-history)
         (when current-prefix-arg 90)))
  (let ((cands (my/journal-search--collect query since-days)))
    (unless cands
      (user-error "日记里没有匹配: %s" query))
    (let* ((choice (if (require 'consult nil t)
                       (consult--read (mapcar #'cdr cands)
                                      :prompt "跳到: "
                                      :sort nil
                                      :require-match t
                                      :history 'my/journal-search-history)
                     (completing-read "跳到: " (mapcar #'cdr cands)
                                      nil t nil 'my/journal-search-history)))
           (target (car (rassoc choice cands))))
      (when target
        (find-file (nth 0 target))
        (goto-char (point-min))
        (forward-line (1- (nth 1 target)))
        (org-reveal)
        (message "%s" (nth 0 target))))))
#+end_src
